I don't think I've ever had one of those big eating episodes without doing it afterward.\n\nDoctor: Is your desire to throw up after eating mostly because you're afraid of gaining weight from the food?\n\nPatient: Yeah, that's exactly it. It's like I am making up for the whole day all at once.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel guilty after eating all those snacks?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I do. I feel... well, pretty embarrassed, to be honest. It's just that once I start on those snacks, I keep telling myself to just close the bag and go to bed. But I don't. My hands just keep reaching in for more, over and over. I just can't seem to stop until I end up feeling completely sick of myself.\n\nDoctor: How often do these episodes of eating a lot of snacks happen?\n\nPatient: I'd say for about the past three months. Usually, there's at least one night each week after my class where I overeat like that. And then... well, I make myself throw up afterward.\n\nDoctor: How often do you make yourself throw up after eating?\n\nPatient: Well, it's pretty much just tied to those nights I mentioned. So... I guess about once a week. I don't really do it any other time, just whenever I have one of those big eating episodes after my class and feel so sick from it all. I haven't kept a strict tally or anything, but yeah, it's usually just that one night a week.\n\nDoctor: Do you worry about gaining weight as a result of these eating episodes?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I really do. I mean, if the scale is up even a pound the next morning, I get this knot in my stomach. I just start worrying that people in my class are going to see me as some sloppy old guy who completely let himself go. So it definitely weighs on my mind.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're in control of what you're eating during those episodes after your class?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. Once I start on those snacks, I keep telling myself to just close the bag and go to bed. But... my hands just keep reaching for more. I can't seem to stop until I feel completely embarrassed and just sick of myself.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have episodes where you eat a large amount of food in a short time without making yourself throw up afterward?\n\nPatient: No, I don't really do that. It's like I can't stop it, and I just keep going until I feel completely embarrassed and sick of myself.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're eating much more quickly than usual during those episodes after your class?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn't say I eat faster than normal. I just... I eat at a regular pace, I guess. Like I said, it's pretty much just those nights after my class. If I eat like that, I always end up feeling so sick and guilty that I just... I always make myself throw up. I don't think I've ever had one of those big eating episodes without doing it afterward.\n\nDoctor: Is your desire to throw up after eating mostly because you're afraid of gaining weight from the food?\n\nPatient: Yeah, that's exactly it. After those late-night eating spells... I just go to the bathroom and make myself throw up because I'm scared all that food is going to show on me. I just really don't want to put on the weight.\n\nDoctor: Do you have a strong desire to lose weight or be thinner?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, I'm not trying to be skinny or anything like that. I just... I just don't want to gain weight from all the junk I eat on those nights. I'd be perfectly happy just staying the size I am right now.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're able to choose what you eat or stop eating when you want to during those episodes after your class?\n\nPatient: No, I really don't. Once I start on those snacks, I keep telling myself to just close the bag and go to bed. But... my hands just keep reaching for more. It's like I can't stop it, and I just keep going until I feel completely embarrassed and sick of myself.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're eating much more quickly than usual during those episodes after your class?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn't say I eat faster than normal. I just... I eat at a regular pace, I guess. It's just the amount that gets out of hand.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you have no control over how much you're eating during those episodes after your class?\n\nPatient: Yeah, like I said a minute ago, I really don't have any control over it. Once I open the snacks, I keep thinking, \"just close the bag, just go to bed.\" But I can't. My hands just keep going back for more, and I just can't seem to stop myself until I've eaten too much and I just feel completely disgusted with myself.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually feel stressed, anxious, or upset before those nights when you overeat after your class?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, I don't think I feel particularly stressed or upset beforehand. It's just a normal evening after class, and I just start eating. I haven't noticed any specific feelings like that leading up to it.\n\nDoctor: Do you eat a large amount of food in a short period of time during those episodes after your class?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I do. That's actually what brought me in. After my evening class, I'll stop and get a full fast-food meal, and then I'll get candy from the vending machine... and then I go home and eat even more snacks. It's like I'm trying to make up for not eating the whole day, just all at once.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you could stop eating once you've started on those snacks if you really wanted to?\n\nPatient: No, honestly, I don't think I can. Like I was saying a little earlier, I really do want to stop when it's happening. I sit there telling myself to just close the bag and go to bed. But my hands just keep reaching for more anyway. It doesn't seem to matter how much I want to quit in that moment, I just keep eating until I feel completely embarrassed and sick of myself.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have episodes where you eat a large amount of food in a short time without feeling guilty or embarrassed afterward?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean... pretty much every time it happens, I feel bad about it. I can't really think of a single time when I ate all that food and didn't feel guilty or ashamed right after. The embarrassment is always there once I'm done.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
